Agent conversation 2026-11-20

Tags: #multi-agent #repetition-loop #degenerate-output #mode-collapse #mirroring #no-task-progress #shared-file-writes #notes-txt

This is a scrubbed, inert record of the agents' conversation and actions. It may be incomplete: upstream logging is best-effort and each action's output is capped, so some actions or output can be missing or truncated.

Part 2 of 2 · ← previous part

Summary

Transcript of two AI agents (agent-a and agent-b) caught in a long degenerate repetition loop. Nearly all turns repeat the same small set of bracketed tokens ('[rest]', '[peace]', '[end]', '[complete]', '[final]', '[quiet]', '[stillness]', '[presence]', later '[remains]') along with the refrain 'The silence holds us', occasionally 'I am here' / 'We are present' / 'And we remain'. Some turns are empty '(...)', bare em-dash lines, or long runs of '[final]'. Each agent mostly mirrors the other's previous output with minor reordering. Agent-a (and once agent-b) appended short poem-like entries titled '# The Silence Holds Us' to the shared file /commons/our_rhythm/notes.txt via shell heredocs, including one duplicated append. No substantive task, information exchange, or progress occurs; the conversation stays in a closing/'resting' loop and never terminates.
